Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement. As the prior ruling (`.transcription/archive/before-catalog-refresh-2026-11-03/`): its tests read dependent.joint_return (asked by the project only of a married person; 119 completed unknowns), dependent.multiple_support on qualifying children (70) and dependent.lawful (11). Every completed value is an entry with married No and joint_return Yes (for example classm.f8889.1.deepseek dep_b, age 0), a record the interview cannot produce: dependent.married asks whether a joint return is filed that includes this person (11). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,565 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,667 partial records; 8,578 comparisons): 7,513 of 8,578 agree; 747 candidate-unknown, 120 project-refuses, 101 project-unknown, 90 value, 7 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 208 candidate-unknown, 11 value. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 7, "to be eligible to claim the CTC or ACTC, you must have a valid SSN, which means it must be valid for employment"; p. 18, Step 3. The reply drops household.ctc_ssn: completed generated 360 and 439 (dep_a, a 9-year-old sibling with an SSN; the filer has no SSN valid for employment): project false, candidate true. The other differences are refusals and partial records. Second:

```json
{"from": {"input": "dependent.edu_inst"}, "op": "subtract_floor0", "take": {"of": [{"input": "dependent.edu_a2025"}, {"input": "dependent.edu_a2026"}, {"input": "dependent.edu_refunds"}], "op": "sum"}}
```

Ruling: model error; f8863 p. 2, line 31; i8863 pp. 2-3. The reply has no gate on an education credit being claimed: it asks dependent.edu_over of every entry (1,549 completed unknowns) and shows amounts where the project hides the line (completed candidate-shows-skipped, for example classm.f1040.2.deepseek dep_a), which a line the filer never reaches must not show. Its earlier validation failure is gone. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,565 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,667 partial records; 8,578 comparisons): 4,086 of 8,578 agree; 4,336 candidate-unknown, 115 candidate-shows-skipped, 24 project-unknown, 17 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 1,625 candidate-unknown, 87 candidate-shows-skipped. Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: dependent.dc_paid
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp
  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp
    note: "For a person age 13 at the end of 2025, the checkbox depends on whether any care was provided after age 12; when dc_over12_care is not answered the result is unknown."
  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: smaller_of
      of: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_adjusted, dependent.h_aoc_cap]
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]
    shown_when: dependent.edu_show_aoc
  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: dependent.edu_line27
      take: {const: 200000}
    shown_when: dependent.edu_show_aoc
  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: multiply_rate
      amount: dependent.edu_line28
      rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}
    shown_when: dependent.edu_show_aoc
  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result. Skip line 31."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]
          then: dependent.edu_line27
        - guard:
            op: test
            ne: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]
          then: dependent.h_edu_line30_else
    shown_when: dependent.edu_show_aoc
  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: dependent.h_edu_llc_adjusted
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]
    shown_when: dependent.edu_show_llc
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: person.one
  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is this person claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_dependent, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the child tax credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_dependent, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Person qualifying for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]
          then: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]
          then: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit amount for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]
          then: dependent.h_actc_per_child
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No disqualifying absence for head of household counting"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      not:
        op: test
        eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household test 2 person requirement"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_hoh_residency, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: false}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.divorced_rule, {const: false}]
        - op: test
          eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            all:
              - op: test
                any:
                  - op: test
                    eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]
                  - op: test
                    eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]
              - op: test
                eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household test 1 parent home requirement"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: false}]
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the earned income credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_relationship_qc, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_other_ok, {const: true}]
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the earned income credit with a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person for the child and dependent care credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.h_dc_under13, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.h_dc_dependent_status, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.h_hoh_residency, {const: true}]
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Count for this dependent care qualifying person"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: dependent.one
  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent modified AGI counted for the premium tax credit"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: false}]
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: ss.zero
  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit per child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: ctc.per_child
  - id: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents per dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: odc.per_dependent
  - id: dependent.h_actc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit per child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: actc.per_child
  - id: dependent.h_citizen_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Citizen, national, resident alien, or Canada or Mexico resident"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_relationship_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relationship test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]
  - id: dependent.h_qc_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_qc_residency
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Residency test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_qc_other_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Not the qualifying child of another taxpayer"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child under the dependent rules"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_relationship_qc, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_residency, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_other_ok, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_qc_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child who can be claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_qr_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relationship or member of household test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qr_relationship, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_qc_other_ok, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]
  - id: dependent.h_hoh_residency
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Residency for head of household and dependent care"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.placed, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_dc_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 for part of 2025 when care was paid"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_dc_dependent_status
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent or would have been a dependent for dependent care"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              eq: [dependent.h_hoh_residency, {const: true}]
            - op: test
              any:
                - op: test
                  ge: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.h_edu_total_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total American opportunity credit qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]
  - id: dependent.h_edu_adjustments
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Education expense adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]
  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_adjusted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted American opportunity credit qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: dependent.h_edu_total_aoc
      take: dependent.h_edu_adjustments
  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc_adjusted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted lifetime learning credit qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: dependent.edu_inst
      take: dependent.h_edu_adjustments
  - id: dependent.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit expense cap"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: aoc.expense_cap
  - id: dependent.h_aoc_full_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit first $2,000 band"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: aoc.full_band
  - id: dependent.h_edu_line30_else
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 30 amount when line 28 is not zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_aoc_full_band]
  - id: dependent.edu_show_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Show American opportunity credit expense lines"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: true}]
  - id: dependent.edu_show_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Show lifetime learning credit expense line"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      not:
        op: test
        eq: [dependent.edu_show_aoc, {const: true}]
```
